<main class="wrap stories"><article class="story top" id="story-49643982" data-id="49643982" data-tags="Programming|Web" data-score="284" data-comments="189" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49643982" data-time="1789049371">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://shopify.engineering/back-to-native" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Shopify moves back to Native from React Native</span><span class='zh'>Shopify 从 React Native 回归原生开发</span></a><span class="dom">shopify.engineering</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Shopify shifts away from React Native back to native app development, citing performance and maintainability benefits.</span><span class='zh'>Shopify 从 React Native 回归原生应用开发，理由包括性能与可维护性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Shopify shifts away from React Native back to native app development, citing performance and maintainability benefits. The move impacts cross-platform tooling strategies and sets a precedent for large-scale mobile apps.</span><span class='zh'>Shopify 从 React Native 回归原生应用开发，理由包括性能与可维护性。此举将影响跨平台工具策略，并为大规模移动应用设立一个新的参照。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">02</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49643556" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Tell HN: OpenAI keeps re-enabling the &#x27;allow training&#x27; setting</span><span class='zh'>告知 HN：OpenAI 不断重新启用“允许训练”设置</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A post claims OpenAI is repeatedly re-enabling the “allow training” toggle after users disable it.</span><span class='zh'>有博文称，即使用户关闭了“允许训练”选项，OpenAI 仍会反复重新启用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A post claims OpenAI is repeatedly re-enabling the “allow training” toggle after users disable it. This raises privacy and data-use concerns, highlighting tensions between user controls, defaults, and how model training data is sourced and managed.</span><span class='zh'>有博文称，即使用户关闭了“允许训练”选项，OpenAI 仍会反复重新启用。这引发隐私与数据使用方面的担忧，凸显用户控制、默认设置以及训练数据来源与管理之间的矛盾。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://rustfoundation.org/media/guest-post-rust-is-tier-1-language-at-microsoft/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Rust Is Tier-1 Language at Microsoft</span><span class='zh'>Rust 已在微软被列为 Tier-1 语言</span></a><span class="dom">rustfoundation.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft formalizes Rust as a Tier-1 language, signaling internal priority for Rust in systems programming and security-focused projects.</span><span class='zh'>微软正式将 Rust 列为 Tier-1 语言，表明在系统编程与安全相关项目中对 Rust 的优先级。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft formalizes Rust as a Tier-1 language, signaling internal priority for Rust in systems programming and security-focused projects. The move could accelerate Rust tooling adoption and influence enterprise engineering practices.</span><span class='zh'>微软正式将 Rust 列为 Tier-1 语言，表明在系统编程与安全相关项目中对 Rust 的优先级。此举可能加速 Rust 的工具链采用并影响企业工程实践。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">07</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://consumerrights.wiki/w/Sony_PlayStation_digital_game_ownership_lawsuit" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>List of references on Sony websites to players &quot;owning&quot; their digital games</span><span class='zh'>索尼网站上关于玩家拥有数字游戏的引用清单</span></a><span class="dom">consumerrights.wiki</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Sony’s site references suggest players own digital games in certain contexts, touching the ownership vs licensing debate in digital rights.</span><span class='zh'>索尼网站对玩家在某些情境下“拥有”数字游戏的表述，触及数字权利中的所有权与许可之辩。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Sony’s site references suggest players own digital games in certain contexts, touching the ownership vs licensing debate in digital rights. The nuances affect ongoing lawsuits and how platforms frame user rights.</span><span class='zh'>索尼网站对玩家在某些情境下“拥有”数字游戏的表述，触及数字权利中的所有权与许可之辩。此类细微表述可能影响相关诉讼与平台对用户权利的界定。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://devblogs.microsoft.com/oldnewthing/20260909-00/?p=112683" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>What algorithm did Windows XP use to choose your initial user picture?</span><span class='zh'>Windows XP 选择初始用户头像所用的算法是什么？</span></a><span class="dom">devblogs.microsoft.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Windows XP didn&#x27;t pick the first user picture at random.</span><span class='zh'>Windows XP 并非随机分配初始用户头像。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Windows XP didn&#x27;t pick the first user picture at random. The article explains the deterministic mapping from a user&#x27;s security identifier to one of a small set of default avatars, a design choice that kept the onboarding lightweight. This seed-based approach yields a reproducible UX with minimal overhead.</span><span class='zh'>Windows XP 并非随机分配初始用户头像。文章揭示了如何使用用户的 SID/名称等身份信息，将其确定性地映射到一组内置头像中的一个，这种设计使新用户的初始体验简单高效。基于种子的一致性策略有助于以低开销实现可重复的用户体验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://rivendell.dmitrybrant.com/relativity/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: What if the speed of light was 5 km/h?</span><span class='zh'>如果光速是每小时5公里？</span></a><span class="dom">rivendell.dmitrybrant.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An interactive thought experiment that asks what if light moved at 5 km/h.</span><span class='zh'>这是一个交互式思考实验，设想光速降至每小时5公里，直观展示在极慢光速下相对论的时间膨胀与 simultaneity 等现象如何显现，并挑战日常直觉。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An interactive thought experiment that asks what if light moved at 5 km/h. The project uses relatable visuals to reveal how relativistic effects would unfold at such a slow speed, challenging common intuition about time, simultaneity, and distance. It highlights why light speed acts as a fundamental cosmic speed limit and how our perceptions would bend if it were slower.</span><span class='zh'>这是一个交互式思考实验，设想光速降至每小时5公里，直观展示在极慢光速下相对论的时间膨胀与 simultaneity 等现象如何显现，并挑战日常直觉。通过可视化演示，帮助理解为何光速成为宇宙中的极限速度，以及在此条件下我们的感知会如何改变。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A reflective note on how adults forget the curiosity of childhood, urging readers to preserve a beginner&#x27;s mindset when tackling problems.</span><span class='zh'>这篇反思文章指出成年人常忘记童年的好奇心，呼吁在解决问题和学习新知识时保持初学者心态。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A reflective note on how adults forget the curiosity of childhood, urging readers to preserve a beginner&#x27;s mindset when tackling problems. It argues that maintaining that sense of wonder is crucial for learning and innovation in math, programming, and technology.</span><span class='zh'>这篇反思文章指出成年人常忘记童年的好奇心，呼吁在解决问题和学习新知识时保持初学者心态。强调持续保持好奇与谦逊有助于在数学、编程与科技创新中保持敏锐与创造力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://spectrum.ieee.org/are-self-driving-cars-safe" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Growing proof that autonomous cars save lives</span><span class='zh'>日益增多的证据表明自动驾驶汽车能拯救生命</span></a><span class="dom">spectrum.ieee.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A growing body of real-world data indicates autonomous vehicles reduce crash rates and save lives as deployment expands.</span><span class='zh'>越来越多的实证数据表明，随着自动驾驶汽车的普及，其发生车祸的概率在下降，从而拯救生命。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A growing body of real-world data indicates autonomous vehicles reduce crash rates and save lives as deployment expands. These findings bolster the case for automation while informing policy, infrastructure, and safety standards.</span><span class='zh'>越来越多的实证数据表明，随着自动驾驶汽车的普及，其发生车祸的概率在下降，从而拯救生命。这些发现为自动化提供了更强的安全论据，并为政策、基础设施和安全标准提供参考。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://openai.com/index/navier-stokes-solution/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/7hiHADoMhgN0iDcje9Xdei/2dae059c10a0dfa3e2814ebb522e9df2/navier-stokes-og.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='openai.com' data-c='#10b981' data-tag='Science'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">20</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://openai.com/index/navier-stokes-solution/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>On the Navier–Stokes Millennium Prize Problem</span><span class='zh'>关于 Navier–Stokes 千禧奖难题</span></a><span class="dom">openai.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Navier–Stokes Millennium Prize Problem asks whether smooth, globally defined solutions exist for the 3D incompressible Navier–Stokes equations with reasonable initial data.</span><span class='zh'>Navier–Stokes 千禧奖难题聚焦三维不可压缩方程在初始条件下是否存在光滑的全局解。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Navier–Stokes Millennium Prize Problem asks whether smooth, globally defined solutions exist for the 3D incompressible Navier–Stokes equations with reasonable initial data. The piece explains why the question is so difficult, what solving it would mean for math and physics, and how researchers brandish partial results and new techniques in attempts to crack it. The prize carries a $1 million award from the Clay Mathematics Institute, underscoring the problem’s prestige and impact.</span><span class='zh'>Navier–Stokes 千禧奖难题聚焦三维不可压缩方程在初始条件下是否存在光滑的全局解。文章解释了该问题为何极其困难、如果解决将对数学和物理带来何种影响，以及研究者如何通过部分结果和新方法推进。该奖项由克莱数学研究所提供一百万元奖金，凸显其极高的学术价值。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A New Yorker feature critiques surveillance-centric tech ecosystems and argues that Flock envisions a world where tracking is ubiquitous and inescapable.</span><span class='zh'>新 Yorker 的专栏批评以监控为核心的技术生态，认为 Flock 想象的是一个无处不在、难以逃脱的监控世界。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A New Yorker feature critiques surveillance-centric tech ecosystems and argues that Flock envisions a world where tracking is ubiquitous and inescapable. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://ai.meta.com/muse/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Muse – Meta’s personal AI agent</span><span class='zh'>Muse：Meta 的个人 AI 助手</span></a><span class="dom">ai.meta.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Muse is Meta&#x27;s attempt to offer a consumer-facing personal AI agent that manages daily tasks, memories, and multi-app workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Muse 是 Meta 推出的个人 AI 助手，旨在帮助管理日常任务、记忆与跨应用工作流。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Muse is Meta&#x27;s attempt to offer a consumer-facing personal AI agent that manages daily tasks, memories, and multi-app workflows. It aims to unify chat, tools, and contextual memory across apps, signaling a shift toward deeply integrated personal AI assistants. Privacy and data-control questions accompany these promises.</span><span class='zh'>Muse 是 Meta 推出的个人 AI 助手，旨在帮助管理日常任务、记忆与跨应用工作流。它体现了个人 AI 助手深度整合日常生活的趋势，同时引发数据隐私与平台控制方面的讨论。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GNU Radio, the long-standing open-source software for software-defined radio, is now playable in a browser.</span><span class='zh'>开源无线电工具 GNU Radio 现可在浏览器中使用，降低入门门槛，便于教育与快速原型开发，但也带来对性能、离线可用性等方面的讨论。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GNU Radio, the long-standing open-source software for software-defined radio, is now playable in a browser. This lowers entry barriers for education and rapid prototyping, while raising considerations about performance and offline access.</span><span class='zh'>开源无线电工具 GNU Radio 现可在浏览器中使用，降低入门门槛，便于教育与快速原型开发，但也带来对性能、离线可用性等方面的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://tautology.town/2026/06/01/card-networks.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>What do Visa and Mastercard do? An intro to card networks</span><span class='zh'>信用卡网络：Visa 与 Mastercard 的作用入门</span></a><span class="dom">tautology.town</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Card networks like Visa and Mastercard provide the rails for almost every consumer payment, handling transaction routing, risk checks, and settlement between banks.</span><span class='zh'>Visa 与 Mastercard 等信用卡网络提供支付主干，负责交易路由、风控和清算等环节。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Card networks like Visa and Mastercard provide the rails for almost every consumer payment, handling transaction routing, risk checks, and settlement between banks. They sit between issuers and merchants, enabling global, near-instant payments with standardized rules.</span><span class='zh'>Visa 与 Mastercard 等信用卡网络提供支付主干，负责交易路由、风控和清算等环节。它们位于发卡行与商户之间，借助统一规则支撑全球近乎即时的支付体验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theverge.com/tech/991190/lg-tv-spying-standby-recording-wi-fi-scanning-gamers-nexus" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>LG TVs caught spying even when offline or on standby</span><span class='zh'>LG 电视在离线或待机状态下仍在窃听</span></a><span class="dom">theverge.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>LG TVs reportedly continue data collection even when powered off or in standby, including firmware scanning and possible gamer-data collection.</span><span class='zh'>LG 电视据称在离线或待机状态下仍在进行数据收集，包括固件扫描和可能的游戏玩家数据收集。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>LG TVs reportedly continue data collection even when powered off or in standby, including firmware scanning and possible gamer-data collection. The privacy implications for IoT devices are significant, highlighting the need for robust on-device controls and clearer user opt-outs.</span><span class='zh'>LG 电视据称在离线或待机状态下仍在进行数据收集，包括固件扫描和可能的游戏玩家数据收集。对物联网设备而言，这一隐私问题相当重要，凸显需要更强的设备内置隐私控制和更清晰的用户退出选项。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Discussion about AI tackling open mathematical problems, framing it as AI &#x27;mining&#x27; an exhaustible supply of open questions.</span><span class='zh'>关于人工智能对开放数学难题的处理，将其形容为在不可再生的难题资源上“开采”。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Discussion about AI tackling open mathematical problems, framing it as AI &#x27;mining&#x27; an exhaustible supply of open questions. The post raises concerns about over-reliance on AI to solve math challenges, and questions how such dynamics affect the mathematical commons, incentives, and long-term research.</span><span class='zh'>关于人工智能对开放数学难题的处理，将其形容为在不可再生的难题资源上“开采”。此趋势引发对AI主导解题可能削减公开问题供给、改变研究激励与数学共同体治理的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.science.org/content/article/how-ai-math-breakthrough-ignited-controversy" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>How An AI math breakthrough ignited a controversy</span><span class='zh'>AI 数学突破引发争议</span></a><span class="dom">science.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A breakthrough in AI-assisted mathematics catalyzed a heated debate over its validity and implications.</span><span class='zh'>AI 辅助数学取得的突破引发激烈争议，核心在于结果的可靠性和方法透明度。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A breakthrough in AI-assisted mathematics catalyzed a heated debate over its validity and implications. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://openreview.net/challenge?redirect=%2Fforum%3Fid%3Dpc7fqaOcAH" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Large language models develop novel social biases through adaptive exploration</span><span class='zh'>大型语言模型在自适应探索中产生新型社会偏见</span></a><span class="dom">openreview.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Adaptive exploration in large language models can surface novel social biases as models adjust to prompts and tasks.</span><span class='zh'>在自适应探索任务空间时，模型输出会显现、甚至改变偏见信号。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Adaptive exploration in large language models can surface novel social biases as models adjust to prompts and tasks. The finding suggests bias is not static but shaped by the model&#x27;s search strategy, with implications for safety, evaluation, and debiasing efforts. The takeaway is to design exploration and prompting policies that minimize biased behavior.</span><span class='zh'>在自适应探索任务空间时，模型输出会显现、甚至改变偏见信号。研究表明偏见并非一成不变，而受探索策略影响，安全与公平评估需将探索动态纳入考量。核心要点是通过审慎设计探索和提示策略来降低偏见风险。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Critique arguing OpenAI lacks mathematicians who can truly understand their outputs, highlighting concerns about mathematical rigor and reliability in AI-generated proofs or formal results.</span><span class='zh'>认为 OpenAI 缺乏真正能理解自己产出证明的数学家，强调对 AI 生成的证明与形式化结果的数学严谨性与可信度问题。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Automattic CEO Matt Mullenweg has been placed on a leave of absence, prompting questions about leadership continuity at Automattic and WordPress.</span><span class='zh'>Automattic 的首席执行官 Matt Mullenweg 已被置于休假状态，引发对 Automattic 与 WordPress 领导力连续性的讨论。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Automattic CEO Matt Mullenweg has been placed on a leave of absence, prompting questions about leadership continuity at Automattic and WordPress. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://buttondown.com/blog/lotus-notes-email" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Lotus Notes and the dangers of starting from scratch</span><span class='zh'>Lotus Notes：从零开始的风险</span></a><span class="dom">buttondown.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Starting from scratch is seductive but risky; legacy systems like Lotus Notes illustrate the hidden costs of abandoning mature platforms.</span><span class='zh'>从零开始的做法往往诱人却风险重重，像 Lotus Notes 这样的遗留系统案例揭示了放弃成熟平台所隐藏的成本与集成挑战。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Starting from scratch is seductive but risky; legacy systems like Lotus Notes illustrate the hidden costs of abandoning mature platforms. The piece argues for leveraging proven, well-integrated foundations rather than reinventing the wheel.</span><span class='zh'>从零开始的做法往往诱人却风险重重，像 Lotus Notes 这样的遗留系统案例揭示了放弃成熟平台所隐藏的成本与集成挑战。文章主张在更新中优先利用经过验证的、已高度集成的基础设施。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ayghri/i-have-adhd" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I-have-ADHD: A skill to stop coding agents from burying the answer</span><span class='zh'>我有 ADHD：阻止编码代理把答案埋起来的技巧</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A personal take on managing ADHD while building AI agents, sharing practical tactics like task structuring, explicit goals, and timeboxing to keep solutions on track.</span><span class='zh'>作者结合 ADHD 的经验，给出在构建 AI 代理时保持聚焦的实用方法，如任务结构化、明确目标与时间盒管理。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Procedural Graphs propose self-evolving execution structures to guide LLM agents, enabling adaptive, task-aware planning.</span><span class='zh'>过程图提出自进化执行结构来引导 LLM 代理，使其在任务中实现自适应、上下文感知的规划。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://quesma.com/blog/qwen38-27b-quantizations-benchmarked/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Benchmarking Qwen3.8 27B quantizations: 4-bit holds up, 1-bit collapses</span><span class='zh'>对 Qwen3.8 27B 量化的基准测试：4 位保持性能，1 位崩溃</span></a><span class="dom">quesma.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New benchmarks show that 4-bit quantization for Qwen3.8 27B preserves accuracy and usability on constrained hardware, while 1-bit quantization collapses performance severely.</span><span class='zh'>最新基准显示，Qwen3.8 27B 的 4 位量化在受限硬件上仍能维持准确性和可用性，而 1 位量化则性能严重下降。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New benchmarks show that 4-bit quantization for Qwen3.8 27B preserves accuracy and usability on constrained hardware, while 1-bit quantization collapses performance severely. The results underscore the trade-offs between model size, speed, and fidelity for deploying large LLMs in resource-limited environments.</span><span class='zh'>最新基准显示，Qwen3.8 27B 的 4 位量化在受限硬件上仍能维持准确性和可用性，而 1 位量化则性能严重下降。结果强调在资源受限环境中部署大模型时，模型规模、速度与保真度之间的权衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://gwern.net/on-really-trying" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>On Really Trying (2009)</span><span class='zh'>真正的尝试（2009）</span></a><span class="dom">gwern.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>What does it mean to really try?</span><span class='zh'>文章探讨真正投入到艰难任务中的含义，分析努力、失败与回报之间的关系，以及在长期项目中动机和资源分配的现实考量。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>What does it mean to really try? The essay probes the psychology of effort, failure, and long-shot projects, challenging the assumption that motivation alone guarantees success. It offers pragmatic takeaways for anyone building ambitious, long-horizon work.</span><span class='zh'>文章探讨真正投入到艰难任务中的含义，分析努力、失败与回报之间的关系，以及在长期项目中动机和资源分配的现实考量。对从事长期、高强度工作的读者具有可操作的启示。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Bespoke proposes a programming language designed around courteous interaction and natural language cues, aiming to lower friction for learners and multilingual teams.</span><span class='zh'>Bespoke 打算用面向礼貌互动和自然语言提示的编程语言，降低学习者和多语言团队的使用门槛，探索社会规范对编码习惯的影响。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Bespoke proposes a programming language designed around courteous interaction and natural language cues, aiming to lower friction for learners and multilingual teams. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://direct.mit.edu/books/oa-monograph/6067/The-Microeconomics-of-Artificial-Intelligence" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The Microeconomics of Artificial Intelligence (2025)</span><span class='zh'>人工智能的微观经济学（2025）</span></a><span class="dom">direct.mit.edu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An open-access monograph analyzing how AI reshapes microeconomics: pricing of AI-enabled goods, productivity effects, firm strategy, data as capital, and competition with winner-take-all dynamics.</span><span class='zh'>一本开放获取的专著，系统分析人工智能如何重塑微观经济学：AI驱动商品定价、生产力与企业策略、数据资本的作用，以及可能的并购与市场集中。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An open-access monograph analyzing how AI reshapes microeconomics: pricing of AI-enabled goods, productivity effects, firm strategy, data as capital, and competition with winner-take-all dynamics. It links theory to policy implications for businesses and regulators. It provides frameworks for evaluating AI adoption and its distributional impacts.</span><span class='zh'>一本开放获取的专著，系统分析人工智能如何重塑微观经济学：AI驱动商品定价、生产力与企业策略、数据资本的作用，以及可能的并购与市场集中。讨论了政策含义、竞争格局和企业在AI时代的投资取舍，为分析AI采用的经济影响提供框架。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.thran.uk/writ/devlog/2026/09/phpbb-144-in-docker.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Getting phpBB 1.4.4 working in Docker</span><span class='zh'>在 Docker 中让 phpBB 1.4.4 正常运行</span></a><span class="dom">thran.uk</span></h3>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://phys.org/news/2026-09-trees-muscle-posture-newly-role.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Tension wood: A &#x27;muscle&#x27; that can both bend and straighten plants</span><span class='zh'>张力木：一种能弯曲也能拉直植物的“肌肉”</span></a><span class="dom">phys.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Tension wood is a specialized tissue in trees that generates contractile forces to adjust posture.</span><span class='zh'>张力木是一种能通过收缩力调节树体姿态的特殊组织。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Tension wood is a specialized tissue in trees that generates contractile forces to adjust posture. Recent work reveals how this biological muscle-like mechanism enables bending and straightening, informing bio-inspired materials and forestry management.</span><span class='zh'>张力木是一种能通过收缩力调节树体姿态的特殊组织。最新研究揭示了其类似肌肉的工作机制如何实现弯曲与拉直，为生物启发材料和林业管理带来启示。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This post compares GCC&#x27;s nested functions (a C-like closure mechanism) with C++ lambdas, touching on syntax, capturing semantics, and portability concerns.</span><span class='zh'>本文比較 GCC 的嵌套函數（類 C 的閉包機制）與 C++ 的 Lambda，討論語法、捕獲語義與可移植性問題。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This post compares GCC&#x27;s nested functions (a C-like closure mechanism) with C++ lambdas, touching on syntax, capturing semantics, and portability concerns. It helps readers weigh when to prefer nested functions or lambdas based on project constraints.</span><span class='zh'>本文比較 GCC 的嵌套函數（類 C 的閉包機制）與 C++ 的 Lambda，討論語法、捕獲語義與可移植性問題。讓讀者在專案條件下衡量何時應選用嵌套函數或 Lambda。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://arstechnica.com/security/2026/09/once-popular-for-attacking-ai-ascii-smuggling-is-embraced-by-spammers/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Microsoft says email spammers are adopting ASCII smuggling</span><span class='zh'>微软称邮件垃圾邮件发送者正在采用 ASCII 走私技术</span></a><span class="dom">arstechnica.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft reports that email spammers are increasingly using ASCII smuggling to evade content filters, leveraging visually similar characters to slip past checks.</span><span class='zh'>微软报告称邮件垃圾邮件发送者正日益使用 ASCII 走私来绕过内容过滤，利用视觉上相似的字符穿过检测。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft reports that email spammers are increasingly using ASCII smuggling to evade content filters, leveraging visually similar characters to slip past checks. This tactic, once seen in AI-targeted attacks, is re-emerging in more campaigns, prompting improved filtering and anomaly detection.</span><span class='zh'>微软报告称邮件垃圾邮件发送者正日益使用 ASCII 走私来绕过内容过滤，利用视觉上相似的字符穿过检测。这一手法此前在针对 AI 的攻击中出现，如今在更多攻击中再次出现，促使加强过滤和异常检测。</span></p>
